This allows us to derive a REAlignment Reward (REAR) that selectively rescales the proportions of these two reward terms. We then show that REAR can be formulated as a linear combination of token-level policy log-probabilities, making it computationally efficient and easy to integrate with various TTS algorithms such as best-of-$N$ sampling and tree search. Experiments show that compared to other test-time baselines, REAR not only enables scalable test-time realignment for preference alignment tasks under diverse user requirements, but also generalizes to mathematical and visual tasks under appropriate preference settings.

</details>


### [108] [OLIVE: View-Augmented Latent Prediction with Waveform Reconstruction for Speech SSL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30356)
*Karl El Hajal,Mathew Magimai. -Doss*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OLIVE是一个联合优化分析和合成目标的自监督语音表示学习框架，通过视图增强的掩码潜在预测和波形重构统一目标，在生成任务、说话人任务和波形重构上表现优异，同时在识别和语义任务上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督语音表示学习方法通常侧重于分析（如掩码预测）或合成（如重构）目标之一，难以在保留信号级信息的同时获得鲁棒的上下文不变表示，从而限制了模型在多样化任务上的泛化能力。

Method: OLIVE联合优化两个目标：1) 波形重构损失，约束早期编码器特征保留信号级信息；2) 视图增强的掩码潜在预测损失，塑造后期上下文表示的不变性。两者在统一框架下联合优化。

Result: OLIVE在生成任务和说话人任务上取得改进，保持识别和语义任务的竞争力，同时提升了波形重构质量，表明联合优化分析-合成目标能支持广泛的下游任务。

Conclusion: 分析目标（掩码潜在预测）与合成目标（波形重构）的联合优化能够互补优势，使语音表示同时兼顾信号保真度和语义不变性，从而提升在多种任务上的泛化能力。

Abstract: We propose Online Latent prediction with Invariant Views and rEconstruction (OLIVE), a self-supervised speech representation learning framework that jointly optimizes analysis and synthesis objectives. OLIVE combines view-augmented masked latent prediction with waveform reconstruction under a unified objective. Reconstruction constrains early encoder features to retain signal-level information, while masked latent prediction shapes later contextual representations toward invariance for robust downstream performance. We show that these objectives enable representations that support a broad range of tasks. In particular, OLIVE improves results on generation and speaker tasks, maintains competitive performance on recognition and semantic tasks, and improves waveform reconstruction.

</details>


### [109] [MOPD: Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Capability Integration in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30406)
*Wenhan Ma,Jianyu Wei,Liang Zhao,Hailin Zhang,Bangjun Xiao,Lei Li,Qibin Yang,Bofei Gao,Yudong Wang,Rang Li,Jinhao Dong,Zhifang Sui,Fuli Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）框架，通过先为每个领域独立训练RL教师，再在学生模型自身rollout上蒸馏集成多领域能力，消除了暴露偏差并提供密集优化信号，在Qwen3-30B-A3B和工业级模型MiMo-V2-Flash上均取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如Off-Policy Finetune效率低下，Mix-RL会损失性能；多能力集成到单个LLM中仍然困难，且跨领域耦合问题严重。

Method: MOPD分为两阶段：1）为每个领域独立运行专用RL，获得一组领域教师模型；2）在学生模型自身的rollout上对这些教师进行蒸馏，从而消除暴露偏差并提供稠密优化信号。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B上，MOPD优于Mix-RL、Cascade RL、Off-Policy Finetune和Param-Merge基线，几乎继承了每个教师的全部能力。已成功部署到工业级前沿模型MiMo-V2-Flash的后训练中。

Conclusion: MOPD是一种高效的多能力集成后训练范式，支持领域教师并行独立开发，消除了跨领域耦合，在实际工业场景中证明了其实用价值。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) rely on reinforcement learning during post-training to push specific capabilities, yet integrating multiple capabilities into one model remains hard. Existing methods, such as Off-Policy Finetune and Mix-RL, are either inefficient or lose performance. In this work, we propose Multi-teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD), a post-training paradigm for combining the capabilities of multiple domain RL teachers: we first run per-domain specialised RL to obtain a set of domain teachers, then distill these teachers into the student on its own rollouts. This eliminates exposure bias and provides a dense optimization signal. On Qwen3-30B-A3B, MOPD outperforms Mix-RL, Cascade RL, Off-Policy Finetune, and Param-Merge baselines, inheriting nearly all of each teacher's capability. MOPD also enables parallel, independent development of domain teachers, removing the cross-domain coupling typical of multi-domain post-training. MOPD has been deployed in the post-training of MiMo-V2-Flash, an industrial-scale frontier model, demonstrating its practical value for capability integration in frontier-scale LLMs.

</details>


### [110] [Field Order Should Not Matter: Permutation-Invariant Embedding Model Fine-Tuning for Structured Metadata Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30473)
*Aivin V. Solatorio,Olivier Dupriez,Rafael Macalaba*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了结构化元数据目录检索中字段序列化顺序对检索质量的影响，发现标准微调会因字段顺序改变导致性能大幅下降（7.4 nDCG@10）。提出置换不变微调（PI-FT），通过对每个记录使用随机采样字段顺序和随机字段dropout，使模型关注字段标签而非位置，将顺序变化惩罚降至0.2点。同时发布了DevDataBench——一个全LLM生成的15语言、覆盖所有指标的基准测试，并证明118M参数的CPU编码器在微调后超越text-embedding-3-large（0.707 vs 0.556 nDCG@10）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI助手和智能体越来越多地作为访问公共数据和统计的媒介，检索步骤决定了回答是否能基于正确的指标或序列。然而结构化元数据目录的检索面临两个问题：1）字段序列化顺序被当作实现细节，但实际上控制着微调后的检索质量；2）使用日志无法为无人搜索过的指标提供训练信号。

Method: 1) 提出置换不变微调（PI-FT）：在数据加载器中，对每个记录使用随机采样的字段顺序和随机字段dropout进行序列化，使模型关注字段标签语义而非位置信息。2) 构建DevDataBench：完全由LLM生成的15语言、多方面的基准测试，覆盖每个指标，包含有依据的、面向方面的查询。

Result: 标准微调在索引重建时字段顺序变化导致7.4 nDCG@10的性能下降；PI-FT将这一惩罚降至0.2点。微调后的118M参数CPU编码器在DevDataBench上超越所有零样本基线，包括text-embedding-3-large（0.707 vs 0.556 nDCG@10），在低资源语言上增益最大。

Conclusion: 字段序列化顺序不是实现细节而是影响检索质量的关键因素；PI-FT以极小代价消除了这一顺序依赖性；对于AI驱动的数据分发场景，使用小模型加针对性微调比通用大模型更有效。

Abstract: We study retrieval over catalogs of structured metadata, where each record is a small schema whose fields answer different kinds of query. Embedding a record with a text encoder first serializes its fields into a string, which forces a choice of field order. We show this choice, usually treated as an implementation detail, silently controls retrieval quality once the encoder is fine-tuned. A standard fine-tune loses 7.4 nDCG@10 points when the index is rebuilt under a different field order, because it reads absolute position instead of the field labels. We propose permutation-invariant fine-tuning ($\textbf{PI-FT}$), which serializes each record under a freshly sampled field order with random field dropout, so meaning binds to the labels rather than to position. The change is about two lines in the data loader; it costs negligible in-distribution accuracy and cuts the order-change penalty to 0.2 points. We study this in the discovery of development statistics, a catalog of nearly 10,000 indicators that should be searchable in many languages by a model small enough to self-host. As AI assistants and agents increasingly mediate access to public data and statistics, this retrieval step decides whether an answer is grounded in the right indicator or series, making discoverability a precondition for disseminating data through AI. Because usage logs cannot provide training signal for indicators no one has searched, we generate the queries instead. $\textbf{DevDataBench}$ is a fully LLM-generated benchmark of grounded, facet-targeted queries across 15 languages, covering every indicator for both training and evaluation. A fine-tuned 118M-parameter CPU encoder outperforms every zero-shot baseline, including $\texttt{text-embedding-3-large}$ (0.707 vs.\ 0.556 nDCG@10), with the largest gains in low-resource languages. We release the benchmark, pipeline, models, and a reusable PI-FT framework.

</details>


### [111] [SIMAX: A Scalable and Interpretable Framework for Multi-Fidelity and Annotated Clinician-Patient Dialogue Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30491)
*Zhuhan Bao,Rui Yang,Bohao Yang,Zhiyi Liu,Sicheng Shu,Ruio Heerschap,Le Li,Doris Yang,Elisabeth Bond,Haoyuan Wang,Nicoleta Economou-Zavlanos,Joshua M. Biro,Matthew McDermott,Nan Liu,Anand Chowdhury,Kai Sun,Kathryn Pollak,Ed Hammond,Chuan Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SIMAX框架，通过预定义临床场景、人物角色和声线条件，生成带有行为注释的受控医患对话模拟数据，为AI驱动的临床沟通编码系统提供可扩展的评估基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境数字记录员的广泛部署驱动了大规模医患对话采集，但人工编码临床沟通数据成本高、一致性差且难以扩展，亟需AI驱动的自动编码系统；然而评估此类系统需要大量真实对话数据及人工标注标签，两者均难以大规模获取。

Method: SIMAX框架从预定义的临床场景、人物角色、声线条件及目标沟通行为出发，生成受控的医患对话。行为控制基于两个编码手册：Global Codebook（整体沟通质量）和WISER Codebook（特定可计数行为）。评估采用自动化质量评估（UTMOS、WV-MOS、WER、CER、CLAP）和人工评估（MOS、临床真实感评分）以及下游通信编码系统示例。

Result: 生成了3,388个跨三个专科的模拟对话；自动评估显示平均UTMOS 3.03、WV-MOS 2.61、WER 0.07、CER 0.05、CLAP余弦相似度0.41；人工评估中位数MOS 4.67、临床真实感评分3.00；下游评估表明SIMAX可评估通信编码系统对行为目标的响应，并揭示某些维度的敏感性不足。

Conclusion: SIMAX能够生成受控且可重复的模拟医患对话，为开发、验证和优化通信编码系统提供数据基础。

Abstract: Background. The widespread deployment of ambient digital scribes is driving large-scale capture of clinician-patient dialogues. Human coding of clinical communication data remains costly, inconsistent, and difficult to scale, motivating AI-driven communication coding systems. However, evaluating these systems requires real-world dialogues and human-coded labels, both hard to obtain at scale.
  Methods. We developed SIMAX (Scalable and Interpretable Framework for Multi-Fidelity and Annotated Clinician-Patient Dialogue Simulation), a framework for generating controlled clinical dialogue data with reference behavioral annotations. SIMAX generates clinician-patient dialogues from predefined clinical scenarios, personas and voice conditions, and target communication behaviors. Behaviors are controlled using two codebooks: the Global Codebook for overall communication quality and the WISER Codebook for specific countable behaviors. We evaluated SIMAX using automated and human quality assessments and an example communication coding system.
  Results. SIMAX generated 3,388 simulated dialogues across three specialties, multiple visit stages, persona characteristics, and accent conditions. Automated assessment showed mean UTMOS and WV-MOS scores of 3.03 and 2.61, WER and CER of 0.07 and 0.05, and CLAP cosine similarity of 0.41, suggesting reasonable speech naturalness, high transcription fidelity, and positive text-audio correspondence. Human evaluation showed a median MOS of 4.67 and a median clinical realism score of 3.00. Downstream evaluation suggests that SIMAX can assess how a communication coding system responds to behavioral targets and reveal insufficient sensitivity in some dimensions.
  Conclusions. SIMAX generates controlled and reproducible simulated clinician-patient dialogues, providing a data foundation for developing, validating, and refining communication coding systems.

</details>


### [112] [Regime-Aware Peer Specialization for Robust RAG under Heterogeneous Knowledge Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30518)
*Bo Wang,Heyan Huang,Yaolin Li,Yanghao Zhou,Jiahao Teng,Ziyi Yang,Ge Shi,Chong Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAPS-DA是一种面向检索增强生成中上下文与参数知识冲突的、基于同尺度专家分治的框架，通过样本级三专家路由（可接/仲裁/抵抗）和token级双选择器，在固定模型规模下实现优于多种基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中检索到的上下文可能与模型的参数化知识存在冲突，且这类冲突在可靠性上呈现谱系分布（从可靠、部分可靠到对抗性上下文）。现有方法通常采用跨可靠性体制的、无差别监督，将不兼容的学习信号混杂在一起，导致训练效果不佳。

Method: 提出RAPS-DA框架，包含两个互补粒度：(1) 样本级：将冲突划分为三种体制（Grounding, Arbitration, Resistance），从同一基座模型训练三个同尺度专家，每个样本硬路由到匹配的专家进行on-policy反向KL监督；(2) token级：双层面选择器利用跨教师不一致性、学生-教师散度和学生熵过滤无信息/不稳定token，提升置信度不匹配token的权重，随着学生成熟逐步聚焦高冲突token。

Result: 在五个冲突场景和两个分布外基准测试中，RAPS-DA超越了所有提示、解码、微调、强化学习和单教师基线方法。

Conclusion: 性能提升源于固定模型规模下的专业化分工，而非更强的教师模型；专家仅在训练阶段存在，部署时学生模型无需体制标签或专家访问权限。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language models by grounding generation in external context. However, it can be fragile when the retrieved context conflicts with the model's parametric knowledge. Such conflicts span a reliability spectrum, ranging from reliable and partially reliable evidence to adversarial context. Existing remedies often handle such heterogeneous conflicts with regime-agnostic supervision, which can conflate incompatible learning signals across reliability regimes. To disentangle these signals, we propose RAPS-DA, a regime-aware peer specialization framework that addresses conflict at two complementary granularities. At the sample level, conflicts are divided into three regimes, including Grounding, Arbitration, and Resistance, with one same-scale peer specialist trained per regime from a shared base model. Each sample is then hard-routed to its regime-matched peer for on-policy reverse-KL supervision. At the token level, a dual-layer selector uses inter-teacher disagreement, student-teacher divergence, and student entropy to filter uninformative or unstable tokens, upweight confidently misaligned ones, and gradually focus supervision on high-conflict tokens as the student matures. Gains stem from specialization at a fixed model scale, not from a stronger teacher, and the peer specialists exist only during training, so the deployed student requires no regime labels or peer access. Experiments on five conflict scenarios and two out-of-distribution benchmarks show RAPS-DA surpasses all prompting, decoding, fine-tuning, RL, and single-teacher baselines.

</details>


### [113] [TRACE: Temporal Relationship-Aware Conversational Entrainment Detection in Dyadic Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30543)
*Sathvik Manikantan Napa Ugandhar,Hao Zhang,Alison Gunzler,Yuzhe Wang,Thomas Thebaud,Georgi Tinchev,Venkatesh Ravichandran,Laureano Moro-Velázquez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了DyadEE数据集（用于检测双人对话中的情感共鸣）和TRACE框架（基于窗口级别的声学嵌入序列建模），在情感共鸣检测任务上达到了97.01%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语音AI智能体的普及，理解对话交互中的情感共鸣变得越来越重要。情感共鸣受社会关系和对话语境影响，但目前缺乏专门用于检测情感共鸣的数据集和方法。

Method: 构建了DyadEE数据集，包含真实情感共鸣对话和通过伙伴交换、情感重合成技术破坏共鸣的合成交互数据。提出了TRACE框架，将双人交互建模为基于情感微调Whisper表示的有序声学嵌入序列，以窗口级别处理交互轨迹。

Result: 在DyadEE数据集上，融入对话上下文和关系信息能提升情感共鸣检测性能，TRACE框架达到了97.01%的最佳准确率。

Conclusion: 对话上下文和关系信息对于情感共鸣检测至关重要，TRACE框架通过交互轨迹建模有效捕捉了双人对话中的情感协调模式。

Abstract: With the proliferation of speech AI agents, understanding emotional entrainment in conversational interaction has become increasingly important. Emotional entrainment is shaped by social relationships and conversational context, influencing affective coordination over time. We introduce DyadEE, a dataset for emotional entrainment detection in dyadic speech interactions, containing both emotionally entrained conversations and synthetic interactions where entrainment is disrupted through partner swapping and emotion resynthesis. We further propose TRACE, a window-level framework that models dyadic interaction as ordered sequences of acoustic embeddings derived from emotion fine-tuned Whisper representations, treating each sample as an interaction trace rather than pooled utterances. Experimental results on DyadEE show that incorporating conversational context and relationship information improves emotional entrainment detection, with TRACE achieving the best accuracy of 97.01%.

</details>


### [114] [Poller: Are LLMs Suitable for Evaluating the Poetry Understanding Task?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30556)
*Shanshan Wang,Derek F. Wong,Jingming Yao,Lidia S. Chao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Poller方法，通过让大语言模型扮演诗人角色来评估中国古诗理解任务，在八个专业维度上显著降低了评估误差（修辞手法降低94.55%，陌生化降低89.53%），弥合了自动化评估与人类专家评估之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动评估方法不适用于现代中国古诗这一独特文学体裁，人工评估虽可靠但成本高且无法大规模应用，因此需要一种既高效又可靠的自动化评估方法。

Method: 提出Poller（Poetry LLM Evaluator）方法，要求LLM扮演具有详细信息的诗歌作者角色，从诗人视角模拟人类评估和判断，并在八个专业维度上对多首诗歌解读进行全面评估。

Result: 在多个LLM上的实验表明，Poller方法有效降低了LLM与人类之间的评估误差，尤其在特定维度上，修辞手法评估误差降低94.55%，陌生化评估误差降低89.53%，传统LLM评估方法无法达到此性能。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了自动化效率与人类专业判断之间的差距，为诗歌相关任务的自动化评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: Traditional automatic evaluation methods have been shown to be unsuitable for modern Chinese poetry because of the distinct nature of this literary genre. Human evaluation remains reliable, but is expensive and not applicable to large-scale data. In this paper, we propose Poller (Poetry LLM Evaluator), a novel method leveraging large language models (LLMs) to evaluate the poetry understanding task. Specifically, our method requires LLMs to play the role of a poem's author with detailed information, thereby emulating human evaluation and judgment by adopting the poet's perspective. We conducted comprehensive experiments on multiple LLMs, evaluating the interpretations of poems across eight specialized dimensions. Experimental results demonstrate that our method effectively reduces the evaluation error between LLMs and humans. Especially for specific dimension evaluation, Poller-based LLMs achieve a 94.55% and 89.53% error reduction for rhetorical techniques and defamiliarization, respectively, compared to baseline methods. These performances are unattainable by conventional LLM evaluation methods. Experimental results from multiple LLMs across various dimensions validate the efficacy of our method. This work bridges the gap between automated efficiency and human expertise, establishing a foundation for automated evaluation in poetry-related tasks.

</details>


### [115] [Morphing into Hybrid Attention Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30562)
*Disen Lan,Jianbin Zheng,Yuxi Ren,Xin Xia,Xuanda Wang,Xuefeng Xiao,Xipeng Qiu,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FlashMorph方法，将混合注意力层选择形式化为预算约束的子集优化问题，通过构建可变形模型、联合优化逐层门控并结合线性化正则化，高效选择保留全注意力的层，在降低选择成本的同时保持强长上下文召回和通用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合注意力模型中层选择方法依赖启发式策略（如固定放置模式或逐层评分），隐含地将层重要性视为孤立的，忽视了全局混合配置下的层间相互依赖效应。

Method: 1) 将混合层选择形式化为预算约束的子集优化问题；2) 构建可变形模型，为每个全注意力层配备转换后的线性注意力分支；3) 冻结所有权重，在合成长上下文检索数据上联合优化逐层门控，引入线性化正则化；4) 在预设预算下离散化门控以实例化混合架构；5) 进行标准logits蒸馏和长上下文微调。

Result: FlashMorph发现的混合配置更有效，在保持强长上下文召回和通用基准性能的同时，显著降低了层选择成本，证明了其有效性、高效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: FlashMorph通过可微优化而非启发式方法进行层选择，能够捕捉层间相互依赖效应，在效率和效果上均优于现有层选择方法。

Abstract: Hybrid attention models improve long-context efficiency by retaining only a subset of full-attention layers and replacing the remaining layers with linear attention. However, the effectiveness of Transformer-to-hybrid conversion critically depends on which layers preserve full attention. Existing hybrid layer selection methods typically rely on heuristic strategies such as fixed placement patterns or layerwise scoring, implicitly treating layer importance as isolated and overlooking the interdependent layer effect under a global hybrid configuration. In this work, we formulate hybrid layer selection as a budget-constrained subset optimization problem. We further propose FlashMorph (Fast LAyer Selection for Hybrid MORPHing), an effective, efficient and scalable layer selection method for Transformer-to-hybrid conversion. FlashMorph first constructs a morphable model by equipping each full-attention layer with a converted linear-attention branch. It then freezes all model weights and jointly optimizes layerwise gates on synthetic long-context retrieval data, with a linearization regularization that encourages the model to rely on linear attention for efficiency. The learned gates are discretized under a preset full-attention budget to instantiate the hybrid architecture, followed by standard logits distillation and long-context finetuning. Extensive experiments show that FlashMorph discovers more effective hybrid configurations, preserves strong long-context recall and general benchmark performance while substantially reducing layer selection cost compared with existing layer selection methods, demonstrating its effectiveness, efficiency, and scalability.

</details>


### [116] [Uncertainty-Aware Generation and Decision-Making Under Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30578)
*Nico Daheim,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究基于贝叶斯决策理论和风险规避决策的不确定性感知决策算法，应用于辅导和自动同行评审任务，通过共形预测提供策略和分数的保证，发现贝叶斯方法优于风险规避规则，后者在模糊性高时可能退化为通用输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在复杂真实任务中应用广泛，但许多任务具有主观性且需要可信输出，而决策算法方面的研究相对不足。

Method: 基于贝叶斯决策理论和风险规避决策，将辅导策略和评审分数的不确定性纳入决策过程，并使用共形预测提供策略和分数上的保证。

Result: 这些算法能提升生成内容的效用，但需谨慎实现；风险规避规则在模糊性高时可能退化为通用输出而降低性能，贝叶斯方法表现更优。

Conclusion: 决策理论技术可改进LLM决策，但仍存在开放挑战待社区解决。

Abstract: With rapidly improving capabilities, Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in many complex real-world tasks. Beyond requiring in-depth knowledge and reasoning skills, many of these tasks exhibit a high degree of subjectivity and require that the outputs of the model can be trusted. While a lot of progress has been made to train better models, decision-making algorithms have received less attention. In this work, we present and evaluate various uncertainty-aware decision-making algorithms based on Bayesian decision theory and risk-averse decision making on the tasks of tutoring and automatic peer reviewing. Concretely, we take uncertainty over tutoring strategies and review scores into account when generating a tutor response or review and use conformal prediction to provide guarantees over strategy and score. We find empirically that these algorithms can improve the utility of the generations but need to be carefully implemented when ambiguity is high. For example, risk-averse rules can degrade performance by optimizing for generic outputs, while Bayesian methods tend to perform better. Our work uses techniques from decision theory to improve LLM-based decision-making and outlines open challenges for the community.

</details>


### [117] [Scaling the Horizon, Not the Parameters: Reaching Trillion-Parameter Performance with a 35B Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30616)
*Lei Bai,Zongsheng Cao,Yang Chen,Zhiyao Cui,Shangheng Du,Yue Fan,Shiyang Feng,Zijie Guo,Haonan He,Liang He,Xiaohan He,Shuyue Hu,Yusong Hu,Songtao Huang,Yichen Jiang,Hao Li,Xin Li,Dahua Lin,Weihao Lin,Fenghua Ling,Dongrui Liu,Zhuo Liu,Runmin Ma,Chunjiang Mu,Haoyang Peng,Tianshuo Peng,Jinxin Shi,Luohe Shi,Boyuan Sun,Zelin Tan,Shengji Tang,Qianyi Wang,Yiming Wu,Yi Xie,Xiangchao Yan,Jingqi Ye,Peng Ye,Fangchen Yu,Jiakang Yuan,Bihao Zhan,Bo Zhang,Chen Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Shuaiyu Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Yiqun Zhang,Junpeng Zhao,Zhijie Zhong,Bowen Zhou,Yuhao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agents-A1，一个35B参数的混合专家智能体模型。通过从扩展长时域轨迹和异构智能体能力两个维度进行智能体时域缩放，结合长时域知识-行动基础设施（平均45K token轨迹）和三阶段训练策略（全领域监督微调、领域级教师模型训练、多教师领域路由在线蒸馏），在多个长时域基准上达到或超越万亿参数模型（如Kimi-K2.6、DeepSeek-V4-pro）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索智能体时域缩放（Agent-Horizon Scaling）的潜力，研究如何通过扩展长时域轨迹和异构智能体能力两个维度，使较小模型（35B）在长时域任务上达到万亿参数模型的性能水平。

Method: 1) 构建长时域知识-行动基础设施，生成平均45K token的智能体轨迹；2) 三阶段训练：全领域监督微调对齐基础模型、领域级教师模型捕获专门知识、多教师领域路由在线蒸馏+显著词汇对齐技术统一六个异构领域。

Result: Agents-A1在SEAL-0 (56.4)、IFBench (80.6)、HiPhO (46.4)、FrontierScience-Olympiad (79.0)和MolBench-Bind (56.8)上取得领先结果；在SciCode (44.3)、HLE (47.6)和BrowseComp (75.5)上保持强竞争力，超越或匹敌Kimi-K2.6和DeepSeek-V4-pro等万亿参数模型。

Conclusion: 35B参数模型通过智能体时域缩放策略可以在长时域任务上达到万亿参数模型的性能水平，为社区提供了一条实用的缩放路径。

Abstract: We introduce Agents-A1, a 35B Mixture-of-Experts Agentic Model that reaches trillion-parameter-level performance by scaling the agent horizon. We investigate agent-horizon scaling from two perspectives: scaling long-horizon trajectories and scaling heterogeneous agent abilities. To support this goal, we build a long-horizon knowledge-action infrastructure that connects external knowledge, actions, observations, and verifier outcomes, producing agentic trajectories with an average length of 45K tokens. Based on this, we train Agents-A1 with a three-stage recipe. First, we perform full-domain supervised fine-tuning to align the base model with broad agentic behaviors. Second, we train domain-level teacher models to capture specialized expertise in each domain. Third, we propose a multi-teacher domain-routed on-policy distillation with salient vocabulary alignment to improve knowledge transfer efficiency across different domains, unifying six heterogeneous domains into one deployable student model. Agents-A1 achieves strong and broad performance for long-horizon agent benchmarks. Compared with 1T-parameter model such as Kimi-K2.6 and DeepSeek-V4-pro, Agents-A1 achieves leading results on SEAL-0 (56.4), IFBench (80.6), HiPhO (46.4), FrontierScience-Olympiad (79.0), and MolBench-Bind (56.8), and remains highly competitive on SciCode (44.3), HLE (47.6) and BrowseComp (75.5). We hope this work provides the community with a practical path for scaling the horizon using a 35B agent that can reach or match the performance of 1T models on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [118] [ReFreeKV: Towards Threshold-Free KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16886)
*Xuanfan Ni,Liyan Xu,Chenyang Lyu,Longyue Wang,Mo Yu,Lemao Liu,Fandong Meng,Jie Zhou,Piji Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有KV缓存压缩方法依赖输入特定的阈值来分配预算，这在开放域场景中难以适用。本文提出无阈值压缩目标，并实现ReFreeKV方法，在13个数据集上验证了其在不损失性能前提下自适应分配预算的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法通常需要针对特定输入/领域预设阈值来达到最佳性能，但这种依赖输入敏感阈值的设计在开放域场景中严重受限——输入涉及不同领域、长度和难度，难以预先选择合适阈值，导致在任意输入上性能大幅下降。

Method: 提出无阈值（threshold-free）KV压缩目标，消除对输入特定阈值的依赖；在此基础上实现ReFreeKV方法，自适应调整预算分配，同时保持完整缓存的性能。

Result: 在13个涵盖不同上下文长度、任务类型和模型规模的数据集上进行了广泛实验，验证了ReFreeKV的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: ReFreeKV作为首个无阈值KV压缩方法，能够在开放域场景下稳健地实现自适应预算分配，消除传统方法对输入特定阈值的依赖，同时保持完整缓存性能。

Abstract: To reduce memory consumption during LLM inference, a handful of methods have been proposed for KV cache pruning. While these techniques can accomplish lossless memory reduction on many datasets, they often hinge on an under-emphasized condition: an input/domain-specific threshold for KV cache budget needs to be pre-determined to achieve the optimal performance. However, such input-sensitive design may be considerably limited in real-world scenarios, as open-domain inputs span diverse domains, lengths and difficulty levels, without clear boundaries for threshold selection. As a result, the dependence of such input-sensitive threshold can be a fundamental limitation that causes large degradation on arbitrary inputs. In this work, we propose a new objective that lifts the threshold constraints for robust KV compression, advocating for "threshold-free" methods that adaptively adjust budget allocation while preserving full-cache performance. We then propose a novel method, ReFreeKV, serving as the first instantiation of this objective. Extensive experiments across 13 datasets with diverse context lengths, task types, and model sizes demonstrate its efficacy and efficiency. Our code is publicly released at https://github.com/Patrick-Ni/ReFreeKV.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Can AI Draw Science? A Benchmark for Evaluating Scientific Figure Generation by Text-to-Image and Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28406)
*Davie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SciDraw-Bench，首个面向科学图表生成的基准测试，涵盖32个结构化任务、8种图表类型和10个学科，通过四维评估（文本保真度、语义正确性、结构质量、惯例遵循）发现领域专用系统SciDraw AI在所有维度上显著优于通用文生图模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成基准（如GenEval、T2I-CompBench、DPG-Bench）仅评估自然图像的组合性、物体计数和逼真度，未能衡量科学图表的关键质量指标：正确且可读的文本标签、实体及关系的忠实呈现、连贯的图表结构以及学科绘图惯例的遵循。

Method: 构建包含32个结构化任务的SciDraw-Bench基准，覆盖8种图表类型和10个学科；每个任务提供自然语言提示和机器可检查的规格说明（包含标签、关系、组件、惯例和负向约束）。提出四维评估协议：文本保真度（基于OCR的标签召回率和字符错误率）、语义正确性（VLM根据规格判断）、结构质量和惯例遵循，并包含元评估协议和评分者间信度分析。

Result: 在涵盖所有8种图表类型的试点实验中，领域专用系统SciDraw AI在每一维度和图表类型上都大幅优于通用文生图基线模型，其中语义正确性和惯例遵循的差距最大；文本保真度对所有系统而言仍是最难的维度。

Conclusion: 科学图表生成需要专门的评估体系，通用文生图模型在处理文本标签和学科惯例方面存在显著不足，领域专用方法更为有效；文本保真度是当前所有系统的共同瓶颈。

Abstract: Text-to-image and multimodal generative models are increasingly used to produce scientific figures such as mechanism diagrams, experimental-design schematics, conceptual frameworks, and graphical abstracts. Yet existing image-generation benchmarks (e.g., GenEval, T2I-CompBench, DPG-Bench) evaluate natural images and measure compositionality, object counting, or photorealism. None of them measure what makes a generated scientific figure usable: correct and legible text labels, faithful depiction of entities and their relations, coherent diagrammatic structure, and adherence to disciplinary drawing conventions. We introduce SciDraw-Bench, a benchmark of 32 structured scientific-figure generation tasks spanning eight figure types and ten disciplines, where each task pairs a natural-language prompt with a machine-checkable specification of required labels, relations, components, conventions, and negative constraints. We propose a four-dimensional evaluation protocol: Text Fidelity (OCR-based label recall and character error rate), Semantic Correctness (vision-language-model judging against the specification), Structural Quality, and Convention Adherence, together with a meta-evaluation protocol and a preliminary inter-judge reliability analysis (human-rating validation is ongoing). We evaluate a domain-specific system, SciDraw AI, against representative general-purpose text-to-image models, and outline a code-to-figure baseline as a planned extension. In a pilot over all eight figure types, the domain-specific system substantially outperforms the general-purpose baselines on every dimension and figure type, with the largest gaps on semantic correctness and convention adherence; text fidelity remains the hardest dimension for all systems.

</details>


### [120] [On the Necessity of a Liquid Substrate for Mesh Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28413)
*Hongwei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在无中心、无共享时钟、无协调器的多智能体网格中，每个智能体必须在不重训练权重的前提下，从异步、不规则到达的观测中在线融合其他智能体的投影。本文证明两个必要条件：1）自适应时间尺度（固定增益滤波器严格次优）；2）估计依赖到达间隔（任何无视间隔的网络在任意宽度和深度下均无法恢复）。这两个条件在连续时间液体类中交汇，LSTM满足前者，固定连续时间滤波器满足后者，多时间尺度液体网络同时满足两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无中心网格中智能体在三个约束（无共享时钟、无重训练、不规则异步观测）同时存在时的最优状态估计问题，证明该条件下固定权重基底的必然局限。

Method: 从自演化潜变量的不规则外生观测模型出发，通过理论推导证明两个必要条件；合成实验验证LSTM、固定连续时间滤波器和多时间尺度液体网络的性能差异。

Result: 1）自适应时间尺度是必要的，固定增益滤波器严格次优；2）估计必须依赖观测间隔，任何无视间隔的网络（任意容量）无法恢复；3）多时间尺度液体网络同时满足两个条件。

Conclusion: 两个必要条件是针对固定权重基底而言的，若允许重训练则可通过其他方式达到该类别；该必要性对网格中每个智能体均成立，构成网格智能的结构性条件。

Abstract: A mesh of sovereign agents has no center: no shared clock, no shared model, and no coordinator to gather data or retrain. Its competence rests on each agent folding the projections its peers emit into a single internal state, online, from observations that arrive at irregular, unscheduled times, on a substrate whose weights it cannot retrain. Any one of these constraints is tractable on its own; folding optimally under all three at once is not. We ask what such a substrate must be, and prove two necessary conditions from one model of a self-evolving latent observed at irregular, exogenous times. Because the latent changes, its optimal estimator is time-varying: an adaptive timescale is necessary, and every fixed-gain filter is strictly suboptimal. And because arrivals are clock-free, the optimal estimate depends on the elapsed gap between them, which no gap-blind network recovers at any width or depth. This second condition is capacity-independent: scale cannot substitute for the missing dependence. The two conditions intersect in the continuous-time liquid class. An LSTM satisfies the first, a fixed continuous-time filter the second, and a multi-timescale liquid network both. Synthetic experiments confirm each: the network attains the timescale, and the separation is computed exactly. The characterization is necessary, not sufficient, and binds fixed-weight substrates: a network free to retrain reaches the class by other means. Proved per agent, the necessity binds every agent of a mesh, a structural condition on mesh intelligence.

</details>


### [121] [Position: RL Researchers Need to Distinguish Between Solving Simulators and Using Simulators as a Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28433)
*Matthew Vandergrift,Esraa Elelimy,Martha White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出强化学习研究中存在两种使用模拟器的不同场景：解决模拟器（将其作为最终目标）和将模拟器作为实际部署学习的代理（代理学习）。两者在约束条件、适用算法和评估指标上有本质区别，混淆这两种场景会导致误导性结论。作者呼吁社区明确区分这两种使用方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习研究中，研究者常以在基准模拟器上取得高性能为目标，但这一目标可能在实践中异化为"解决模拟器本身"，而非真正关注在部署环境中学习的通用能力。这两种目标本质不同，却未得到明确区分，导致研究方法论和结论的混淆。因此需要澄清并呼吁社区关注这一区别。

Method: 通过理论分析和实验验证相结合的方式：首先从概念上阐述两种模拟器使用场景在约束条件、算法选择和评估指标上的差异；然后通过具体示例和简单实验，展示不区分这两个场景可能引发的若干问题和误导性结论。

Result: 论文识别并展示了不区分"解决模拟器"与"模拟器作为代理学习"两种场景所导致的具体问题，包括算法选择不当、评估标准错位、结论误导等，并通过实验证据加以佐证。

Conclusion: 强化学习社区应当明确区分模拟器的两种使用场景，并针对各自场景采用相应的实验规范和评估标准，以避免误导性结论，推动研究向更清晰的方向发展。

Abstract: One goal in reinforcement learning (RL) research is to understand general-purpose sequential decision-making, using benchmark simulators as a proxy for learning in deployment settings. When running experiments, however, the goal of achieving high performance in the simulator can mutate into focusing exclusively on solving the simulator. To achieve high scores, researchers may adopt solutions exclusively meant for solving simulators, rather than learning while the agent is deployed outside a simulator. Solving simulators is also worthy of investigation, but it is a fundamentally different RL research question. In this paper, we argue that RL researchers need to distinguish between two use cases of simulators: solving simulators and using simulators as a proxy for learning in deployment. We first discuss how these two use-cases are importantly different, in terms of constraints on how the agent can use the simulator, which algorithms are appropriate, and which evaluation metrics are appropriate. We then highlight several issues and misleading conclusions that can occur by not making the distinction between these two settings clear, supported with examples and simple experiments. This work is a call to the community to begin clearly distinguishing how they are using simulators in their work, hopefully sparking further discussion on which empirical practices work best in each setting.

</details>


### [122] [Learning to Distributedly Estimate under Partially Known Dynamics: A Covariance-Agnostic Neural Kalman Consensus Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28441)
*George Stamatelis,Kyriakos Stylianopoulos,George C. Alexandropoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CA-NKCF（协方差无关神经卡尔曼共识滤波器）的新型在线分布式感知框架，它结合了部分领域知识与深度神经网络，在没有噪声统计信息的情况下，通过先验估计、优化共识权重和卡尔曼式递归更新进行分布式推理。实验表明，在多种环境中（线性、混沌Lorenz系统、无线跟踪），CA-NKCF优于传统分布式卡尔曼滤波、粒子滤波以及纯无模型深度神经网络，且对噪声水平、通信拓扑、状态维度和观测杂波密度具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线隐状态估计是人工智能领域的基础挑战，现有方法要么依赖已知噪声统计（如卡尔曼滤波），要么缺乏领域知识整合能力（如纯深度网络），且分布式场景下协作推理困难。本文旨在设计一种无需噪声统计知识、兼具领域知识与深度学习表征能力、且适用于分布式协作的在线隐状态估计框架。

Method: 提出的CA-NKCF框架将先验估计、优化共识权重和卡尔曼式递归更新相结合，利用深度神经网络补充缺失的噪声统计信息和模型校正能力。具体而言，通过共识机制融合多智能体局部信息，使用神经网络自适应学习卡尔曼增益等关键量，实现完全模型无关的分布式推理。

Result: 在多种环境（线性系统、混沌Lorenz系统、实际无线跟踪场景）下，CA-NKCF在估计精度上显著优于传统分布式卡尔曼滤波、粒子滤波以及纯无模型深度神经网络。性能优势在变化噪声水平、随机通信拓扑、不同状态维度和杂波密度条件下保持稳定，且对模型误指定具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CA-NKCF成功融合了领域知识与数据驱动方法的优势，在无需噪声统计信息的分布式场景中实现了鲁棒且准确的在线隐状态估计，为分布式感知系统提供了一种有前景的通用框架。

Abstract: Online latent state estimation constitutes a fundamental challenge within the artificial intelligence field, serving as a foundational tool for diverse applications, including sequential decision making, anomaly and change-point detection. In this paper, a novel online distributed sensing framework, where agents collaborate and exchange information to perform latent state estimation, is presented. The proposed estimator combines available partial domain knowledge with the representation capabilities of deep neural networks. In particular, the designed sensing framework incorporates prior estimates, optimized consensus weights, and Kalman-like recursive updates to perform decentralized inference, without relying on knowledge of noise statistics. Extensive experiments on linear, chaotic (Lorenz), and practical wireless tracking environments reveal that the proposed Covariance-Agnostic Neural Kalman Consensus Filter (CA-NKCF) outperforms traditional distributed Kalman and particle filters as well as purely model-free deep neural networks, exhibiting robustness even when the underlying motion and observation models are misspecified. It is also demonstrated that CA-NKCF's performance advantage remains stable across varying noise levels, random communication topologies, latent state dimensions, and observation clutter densities induced by scattering objects in wireless systems.

</details>


### [123] [S-GAI: Spectral Geometry-Aware Initialization for Sigmoidal MLPs -- From Dataset Geometry to Network Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28444)
*Yi-Shan Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出S-GAI初始化框架，利用SVD从图像数据中提取类谱几何结构来初始化单隐层sigmoid MLP的权重，使网络从一开始就编码了数据分布的几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典普适逼近定理只说明了sigmoid MLP的表达能力，但未指导如何初始化权重以编码数据分布的几何结构。现有随机初始化（如Xavier）无法在训练开始前嵌入数据几何信息。

Method: 对每个类别执行SVD，提取均值、主方向和谱尺度；通过能量阈值选择保留的主方向，每个保留方向用两个sigmoid门表示；所有类别的门组成共享的隐藏层，直接从训练集初始化。同时构建基于SVD的子空间分类器作为非神经网络的几何参考。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和CIFAR-10上，S-GAI初始化的MLP比Xavier初始化从更信息丰富的隐藏状态开始，全训练后达到相近的最终精度；冻结隐藏层仅训练输出层时，性能显著优于冻结随机门。

Conclusion: S-GAI有效地将类谱几何嵌入到MLP的隐藏层中，提供了比随机初始化更好的起点，且几何信息的嵌入在训练过程中得到保留和利用。

Abstract: Classical universal approximation theorems establish the expressive power of sigmoidal multilayer perceptrons, but they do not prescribe how initial weights should encode the geometry of a data distribution. We propose S-GAI, a spectral geometry-aware initialization framework for one-hidden-layer sigmoidal MLPs. Starting from the constructive idea that sigmoid units can act as smooth half-space gates, we move from hand-specified planar geometry to class-wise spectral geometry estimated from image data. For each class, SVD provides a mean, principal directions, and spectral scales. An energy threshold selects the retained directions, and each retained direction is represented by two sigmoid gates. These class-specific gates form a shared hidden layer initialized directly from the training set. We also formulate a SVD-based subspace classifier as a non-neural geometric reference, which tests whether the estimated spectral class geometry is already discriminative before being embedded into the MLP. Experiments on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and a more challenging CIFAR-10 test show that the S-GAI-initialized MLP starts from a substantially more informative hidden state than Xavier initialization and reaches comparable final accuracy under full training. When the hidden layer is frozen, training only the output layer still gives stronger performance than frozen random gates, providing evidence that S-GAI effectively embeds class-wise spectral geometry into the MLP.

</details>


### [124] [scKDGM: KAN-guided Dynamic Graph Masked Learning for Single-Cell RNA-seq Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28459)
*Jun Tang,Pengwei Hu,Sicong Gao,Jie Guo,Lun Hu,Xin Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出scKDGM，一种基于KAN引导的动态图掩码学习框架，用于单细胞RNA-seq聚类，通过GDP-Mask掩码、TAKGCN编码器、动态图构建和跨视图对比学习，在12个真实数据集上优于10个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: scRNA-seq聚类中，高维度、稀疏性、dropout和技术噪声阻碍了鲁棒的表达表示和细胞图构建；现有掩码自编码器主要使用表达恢复进行特征重建，而图聚类方法通常依赖固定KNN图，未将恢复的表达反馈到图优化中。

Method: 提出scKDGM框架，包含：(1) 图感知分布保持基因掩码(GDP-Mask)扰动细胞身份；(2) KAN-based TAKGCN编码器学习掩码视图表示；(3) 掩码引导的表达恢复构建动态图；(4) 跨视图对比学习将恢复信号传递到拓扑更新；(5) ZINB损失建模过度离散和零膨胀。

Result: 在12个真实scRNA-seq数据集上，scKDGM在平均NMI和ARI指标上优于10个基线方法。

Conclusion: 验证了将掩码表达恢复与动态图优化相结合的有效性，为scRNA-seq聚类提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) clustering is essential for identifying cell types, but high dimensionality, sparsity, dropout, and technical noise hinder robust expression representation and cell graph construction. Existing masked autoencoders mainly use expression recovery for feature reconstruction, while graph clustering methods usually depend on fixed KNN graphs and do not feed recovered expression back into graph optimization. We propose scKDGM, a KAN-guided dynamic graph masked learning framework for scRNA-seq clustering. scKDGM uses graph-aware distribution preserving gene masking (GDP-Mask) to perturb cell identity, a KAN-based TAKGCN encoder to learn masked-view representations, mask-guided expression recovery to construct a dynamic graph, and cross-view contrastive learning to transfer recovery signals into topology updates. A ZINB loss models overdispersion and zero inflation. Experiments on 12 real scRNA-seq datasets show that scKDGM outperforms 10 baselines in average NMI and ARI.

</details>


### [125] [Counterfactual Residual Data Augmentation for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28460)
*Hossein Mohebbi,Oliver Schulte,Ke Li,Pascal Poupart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CRDA的反事实残差数据增强技术，用于表格回归任务。该方法利用残差不变性生成新样本来扩充训练集，能显著降低MLP回归器和XGBoost回归器的MSE，分别降低22.9%和6.4%，在多种数据集上优于现有数据生成和增强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的回归任务常面临训练样本有限、采集成本高和观测噪声大等问题，现有数据增强技术主要针对视觉和语言领域，表格回归领域缺乏有效的数据增强方法。

Method: 利用回归器建模数据系统成分后的残差在精心挑选特征的微小扰动下保持不变的特性，基于此残差不变性生成反事实新样本，从而扩充数据集。该方法为模型无关的通用技术。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，CRDA使MLP回归器MSE平均降低22.9%，XGBoost回归器MSE平均降低6.4%；与现有SOTA数据生成器和增强技术相比，CRDA在MSE降低方面持续表现更优。

Conclusion: CRDA通过在训练数据中添加有原则的反事实变体，为噪声多、小样本的回归场景提供了一种简单高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Data-driven modeling in real-world regression tasks often suffers from limited training samples, high collection costs, and noisy observations. Inspired by the impact of data augmentation in vision and language, we propose a novel Counterfactual Residual Data Augmentation (CRDA) technique for tabular regression. Our key insight is that once a regressor has modeled the systematic component of the data, the remaining noise can be viewed as an invariant residual that remains stable under small perturbations of carefully selected features. We exploit this residual invariance to generate new, yet realistic, training samples, effectively expanding the dataset without requiring additional real data. Our method is model-agnostic and readily applicable to various types of regressors. In experiments across datasets from a variety of benchmark repositories, on average, CRDA reduces an MLP Regressor's MSE by 22.9% and an XGBoost Regressor's MSE by 6.4%. When compared to existing state-of-the-art data generators and augmentation techniques, CRDA consistently outperforms in MSE reduction. By adding principled counterfactual variations to the training data, our method offers a simple and efficient remedy for noise-prone, small-sample regression settings.

</details>


### [126] [Singular Learning and Occam's Razor in Deep Monomial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28464)
*Kathlén Kohn,Giovanni Luca Marchetti,Farhan Shabir,Vahid Shahverdi,Weisheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过多项式代数工具（如Mason定理）研究具有单项式激活函数的深度全连接网络，证明当激活函数次数足够大时，临界点恰好出现在子网络处（即部分神经元不活跃或冗余的参数配置），为深度学习中的隐式偏向提供了数学视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络优化中梯度动力学受架构临界点影响的内在机制，这些临界点对应模型参数化Jacobian秩不足的位置，是奇异学习理论中研究的最显著奇点。希望通过数学工具解释深度网络倾向于收敛到更简单函数的隐式偏向。

Method: 采用多项式代数工具，特别是Mason定理，分析具有单项式激活函数的深度全连接网络中的临界点结构。

Result: 对于激活函数次数足够大的深度全连接网络，临界点恰好出现在子网络处，即部分神经元不活跃或冗余的参数配置。

Conclusion: 该结果为深度神经网络的隐式偏向提供了数学视角，解释了模型倾向于收敛到更简单函数的现象。

Abstract: In the optimization of neural networks, gradient dynamics are influenced by critical points that arise from the model's architecture. These critical points occur where the Jacobian of the model's parametrization is rank-deficient, and are the most pronounced singularities studied in Singular Learning Theory. We investigate such points in deep fully-connected networks with monomial activations via tools from polynomial algebra such as Mason's Theorem. We show that, for sufficiently large activation degree, criticality occurs precisely at subnetworks, i.e., at parameter configurations where some neurons are inactive or redundant. This offers a mathematical perspective on the implicit bias in deep neural networks, explaining the tendency of these models to converge toward simpler functions.

</details>


### [127] [An Agentic AI Pipeline for Appliance-Level Energy Anomaly Detection and LLM-Driven Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28467)
*Dihia Falouz,Aida Douaibia,Amine Bechar,Youssef Elmir,Abbes Amira,Adel Oulefki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个端到端智能体流水线，结合深度时间序列预测、变分异常检测和LLM推理，为办公楼设备生成带优先级的可操作维护建议。动态检索策略在保持全量检索性能的同时，将上下文来源从6个削减至3-6个，7B本地模型在所有16个场景中均通过测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 办公楼非专家设施管理员难以有效利用设备级能源监控产生的噪声警报，需要一种能将原始监测数据转化为优先级明确、可执行维护建议的自动化系统。

Method: 混合SSA-LSTM预测模型 + 逐设备LSTM注意力VAE异常检测 + 三阶段LangChain流水线（上下文/诊断/报告Agent）+ 反射性记忆层 + 动态检索策略。

Result: 动态检索与全量静态检索在所有后端上性能相当，上下文来源从6个降至3-6个；最佳后端得分90.4/100，70分阈值通过率100%；7B参数本地模型通过全部16个场景。

Conclusion: 所提端到端流水线能有效将噪声监测数据转化为可操作维护建议，动态检索策略在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅降低计算开销，小规模本地模型即可胜任该任务。

Abstract: Appliance-level energy monitoring in office buildings produces noisy alerts that non-expert facility managers struggle to use. This paper proposes an end-to-end agentic pipeline that combines deep time-series forecasting, variational anomaly detection, and LLM-based reasoning to generate prioritized, actionable maintenance recommendations. The system tracks seven office appliances using a hybrid Singular Spectrum Analysis (SSA) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) forecasting model, and applies a per-appliance LSTM Variational Autoencoder (VAE) with attention to flag abnormal daily consumption episodes. A three-stage LangChain pipeline begins with a Context Agent that always retrieves three core RAG sources (model reliability, hourly baseline, and expert knowledge) and conditionally adds up to three more (forecast context, anomaly history, global baseline) based on event characteristics, capped at eight reasoning steps. A Diagnosis Agent converts the evidence into a structured JSON diagnosis, and a Report Agent renders a human-readable narrative. A reflective memory layer incorporates operator feedback. The dashboard shows real-time 30-minute forecasts, intraday consumption, the previous day anomaly report, and a feedback form. We evaluate the forecasting model, anomaly detector with appliance-specific thresholds, and LLM reasoning on a 16-scenario benchmark including sustained and transient spikes, unexpected shutdowns, and systemic events, comparing five LLM backends under static vs. dynamic retrieval. Dynamic retrieval matches full static retrieval across all backends while cutting average context from six to three-six sources per event. The best backend scores 90.4/100 with a 100% pass rate at a 70-point threshold, and a fully local 7B-parameter model passes all 16 scenarios.

</details>


### [128] [Modelling Emotional Memory in Children with Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28470)
*Henry Groves,Lucia F. Jackson,Barbara-Anne Robertson,Jonte R. Hance*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了情绪效价如何影响儿童识别记忆的顺序依赖结构：儿童对一系列情绪玩具的正确回忆不仅取决于某个玩具本身的效价，还取决于其前后玩具的效价。经典张量网络模型引入效价后达到了77.98%的准确率，展示了量子启发方法在建模顺序依赖现象（如情绪记忆）中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准心理学模型虽然确认了顺序依赖在不同事件间存在差异，但准确率低，且无法反映一个情绪对象如何影响集合中其他对象的记忆。因此需要更有效的建模方法来理解情绪记忆中的顺序依赖现象。

Method: 采用经典张量网络模型，将情绪效价作为因子引入模型中，对儿童识别记忆的顺序依赖结构进行建模。

Result: 经典张量网络模型在建模实验结果时达到了77.98%的准确率，相比标准心理学模型有大幅提升。

Conclusion: 尽管不是严格的"量子认知"模型，但量子启发方法对建模顺序依赖现象（如情绪记忆）具有显著价值。同时，本文引入的任务协议为使用经典和类量子认知模型分析儿童情绪时间记忆提供了新颖的现实工具。

Abstract: We demonstrate how emotional valence influences the order-dependent structure of children's recognition memory: correct recall of a sequence of emotionally-valenced toys depended not just on the valence of a given toy itself, but also on the valence of the toys shown before and after it. Whilst standard psychological models confirm that order-dependence differs across an event (a set of toys shown in sequence), accuracy is low and the model does not reflect how memory for an emotional object influences others in the set. A classical tensor network model factoring in valence is able to achieve a 77.98\% accuracy in modelling the results of the study. While not strictly a ``quantum cognition'' model, this massive increase in accuracy shows the value of quantum-inspired methods for modelling order-dependent phenomena, such as emotional memory. Further, the task protocol we introduce presents a novel, real-world tool for exploring emotional temporal memory in children for analysis using classical and quantum-like models of cognition.

</details>


### [129] [A Trainable-by-Parts Operator Learning Framework: Bridging DeepONet and Karhunen-Loeve Expansions for Large-Scale Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28519)
*Christian Munoz,Alexandre Tartakovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于KL-DNN的可扩展算子学习框架，用于大规模PDE问题（如地质碳存储）。在170万网格单元、50时间步的3D数据集上训练，通过低秩SVD和嵌套KL展开构建潜空间，实现全分辨率预测。压力RMSE为1.1 psi (0.04%相对误差)，CO2饱和度RMSE为0.0146 (5%相对误差)，单GPU训练20分钟，相比DeepONet误差降低7-19%，速度快两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模PDE问题中算子学习面临维度灾难、内存限制和训练数据稀缺三大挑战，现有方法（如DeepONet）在精度和效率上仍有不足，亟需可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 基于KL-DNN框架：对静态属性采用低秩SVD构建潜空间，对动态压力场采用嵌套Karhunen-Loeve展开，实现全分辨率预测而无需子采样或空间粗化。

Result: 压力RMSE 1.1 psi (0.04%相对误差)，CO2饱和度RMSE 0.0146 (5%相对误差)；单GPU训练20分钟；相比DeepONet压力误差降低19%，饱和度误差降低7%，速度提升两个数量级；单次推理<1分钟。

Conclusion: KL-DNN可作为大规模PDE问题的实用高精度求解方案，支持快速不确定性量化、历史匹配和实时决策支持。

Abstract: Training operator-learning models for large-scale problems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) is challenging due to the curse of dimensionality, memory constraints, and limited training data. These challenges arise in many scientific and engineering applications, including subsurface flow, climate modeling, and geological carbon storage (GCS). In this work, we propose a scalable operator-learning framework based on the Karhunen-Loeve Deep Neural Network (KL-DNN) and demonstrate its performance for modeling GCS. The model is trained on a dataset comprising 100 samples of large-scale simulations in a three-dimensional domain with 1.7 million cells and 50 time steps. The KL-DNN method constructs latent spaces using low-rank singular value decomposition of static properties and a nested Karhunen-Loeve expansion for dynamic pressure fields, enabling full-resolution predictions without subsampling or spatial coarsening. The KL-DNN model achieves an average root mean square error (RMSE) of 1.1 psi for pressure (0.04% relative error with respect to the average pressure in the domain) and RMSE of 0.0146 for CO2 saturation (5% relative error with respect to the average saturation inside the plume). The model requires 20 minutes of training on a single GPU, representing a 19% reduction in the pressure errors, 7% reduction in the saturation error, and a two-order-of-magnitude speedup compared to DeepONet trained on the same dataset. These results, along with inference time of less than one minute, establish the proposed model as a practical and accurate solution for large-scale PDE problems, enabling rapid uncertainty quantification, history matching, and real-time decision support.

</details>


### [130] [A Gravitational Interpretation of Fine-Tuning Reversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28525)
*Samuele Poppi,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“微调回归的引力解释”假说：早期大规模训练形成主导行为流形，后期对齐/微调只是在浅层偏离该流形，导致后续微调会沿着历史定义的回归方向（v_rev）发生回退。实验表明，通过选择性地阻断v_rev方向上的移动，可以有效减少有害性（从19.0%降至8.5%），且任务性能损失很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调无害数据会部分逆转前期训练习得的行为，导致安全性退化、遗忘能力重现、潜在特征通过无关监督迁移等现象。作者认为这些现象存在共同根源，需要一个统一的解释框架。

Method: 提出“引力解释”几何假说：早期大训练阶段形成主导行为流形，后期对齐/专门化阶段是该流形的浅层位移。发现代表漂移会快速获得沿历史定义回归方向（v_rev）的分量；通过实验验证v_rev与回归行为的相关性，并采用阻断v_rev方向运动的方法进行因果干预。

Result: 主实验中，对齐度（与v_rev的余弦相似度）从第一步的0.429±0.052上升到第20步的0.647±0.021；24个运行-步数对中，所有观测值均超过各向同性激活空间零假设的p99。阻断v_rev方向运动后，T=100步的对齐度从0.648±0.009变为-0.211±0.021，有害性从19.0%±4.0%降至8.5%±1.5%，任务性能损失很小。

Conclusion: v_rev是早期对齐后回退的因果相关中介方向，但并非唯一的安全方向，主导流形也非直接可观测。该方向能解释并部分控制早期回归动态。

Abstract: Fine-tuning on harmless data can partially undo behaviors acquired earlier in training. Safety can erode under benign post-alignment updates, unlearned capabilities can re-emerge, latent traits can transfer through apparently unrelated supervision, and related post-alignment fragility appears in other generative settings. We argue these phenomena are usefully viewed through a common training-history lens. Our hypothesis is geometric: large early training phases create dominant behavioral manifolds, while later alignment or specialization phases are shallower displacements from them. Subsequent fine-tuning can therefore inherit a persistent reversion component pointing back toward a witness of the dominant manifold. We call this the gravitational interpretation of fine-tuning reversion. Across our main settings, representational drift rapidly acquires a component along a history-defined reversion direction (v_rev). In our main track, alignment with v_rev rises from cos = 0.429 +/- 0.052 after the first update to 0.647 +/- 0.021 by step 20. Across 24 run-step pairs, every observed alignment exceeds the p99 of an isotropic activation-space null. We demonstrate that selectively blocking motion along v_rev changes the final alignment at T=100 from 0.648 +/- 0.009 to -0.211 +/- 0.021 and reduces harmfulness from 19.0% +/- 4.0% to 8.5% +/- 1.5% with little task cost. These results support v_rev as a causally relevant mediator of early post-alignment reversion in our setup. Importantly, we do not claim that v_rev is the unique safety direction, nor that the dominant manifold is directly observed; rather, we identify a robust, history-defined direction that explains and partially controls early reversion dynamics.

</details>


### [131] [NIVA: A Multimodal Foundation Model for Actionable Earth System Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28546)
*Anisha Pal,Aodhan Sweeney,Kyle Heyblom,Kalai Ramea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NIVA是一个多模态基础模型，通过学习地球系统组件（如海洋和大气）的统一表示，旨在实现次季节到季节尺度的预测。在双模态（海洋-大气）验证中，模型能够捕获耦合动力学特征，并准确预测主要气候指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法难以建模耦合的地球系统动力学，限制了超越~2周可预测性窗口的能力，因此需要一种能够学习地球系统组件间统一表示的基础模型。

Method: 提出NIVA多模态基础模型，在大规模地球系统模拟数据上训练，学习跨模态的物理意义结构。本文聚焦于海洋-大气双模态作为受控概念验证。

Result: NIVA能够捕捉关键的气候变率模态，并通过准确预测主要气候指数得到初步验证。

Conclusion: NIVA验证了基础模型可以学习耦合动力学，为次季节到季节预测提供了基础框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI-driven weather and climate modeling have improved forecast skill while reducing computational cost. However, existing data-driven approaches are limited in their ability to model coupled Earth system dynamics, which is required for extending predictability beyond the ~2-week horizon. To address this, we introduce NIVA, a multimodal foundation model designed to learn unified representations across Earth system components. While the full framework targets atmosphere, ocean, ice, and land interactions, we focus here on a two-modality setting (ocean and atmosphere) as a controlled proof of concept to evaluate whether foundation models can learn coupled dynamics. Trained on large-scale Earth system simulations, NIVA learns physically meaningful cross-modal structure, providing a foundation for subseasonal-to-seasonal prediction. As initial validation, we show that NIVA captures key modes of climate variability through accurate prediction of major climate indices.

</details>


### [132] [Improving Coherence in Hierarchical Time Series Forecasting using Structured Temporal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28553)
*Ruchi Pakhle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出层次化时序融合模型（HTF），通过在训练目标中嵌入一致性损失函数，在保持预测精度的同时自动实现层次结构间的预测一致性，优于传统后处理方法和深度学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次结构下的时间序列预测需要同时满足预测准确性和一致性（低层预测之和等于高层预测），传统统计方法（如Bottom-Up、MinT）通过后处理强制一致性，但无法建模复杂的非线性时序依赖和协变量交互。

Method: 在Temporal Fusion Transformer（TFT）基础上扩展，引入结构化层次嵌入和一致性感知损失函数。训练时直接惩罚子节点预测聚合与父节点预测之间的差异，使模型同时学习时序动态和结构一致性。

Result: 在M5 Walmart和层次化能源消耗数据集上，HTF显著降低预测不一致性，同时提升预测精度，优于经典协调方法和深度学习基线。注意力可视化和嵌入分析揭示了时序与结构信息对层次预测的贡献。

Conclusion: 将一致性约束直接嵌入训练目标是实现层次预测一致性的有效途径，HTF框架在不牺牲准确性的前提下解决了层次一致性问题，并提供了可解释性分析。

Abstract: In many real-world applications, such as retail sales, energy usage, and supply chain planning, forecasting is performed across hierarchical structures. These structures often represent aggregations (e.g., products to categories to regions), where forecasts must not only be accurate but also coherent, meaning that lower-level predictions sum correctly to higher-level forecasts. Traditional statistical methods, such as Bottom-Up and MinT, enforce coherence through post-processing but fail to model complex nonlinear temporal dependencies and covariate interactions.
  We propose Hierarchical Temporal Fusion (HTF), a novel extension of the Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) that integrates structured hierarchical embeddings with a coherence-aware loss function to ensure consistent forecasts across all levels of a hierarchy. Rather than applying reconciliation after forecasting, HTF embeds coherence directly into the training objective. The coherence loss penalizes the difference between aggregated child forecasts and their corresponding parent forecasts during training, enabling the model to learn both temporal dynamics and structural consistency simultaneously.
  We evaluate HTF on two publicly available benchmark datasets: the M5 Walmart forecasting dataset and a publicly available hierarchical energy consumption dataset. Results demonstrate that HTF substantially reduces forecast incoherence while improving forecasting accuracy compared with classical reconciliation methods and deep learning baselines. In addition, attention visualization and embedding analysis provide insight into how temporal and structural information contribute to hierarchical forecasting performance.

</details>


### [133] [Geometric Measurements of the Axiom of Choice in Neural Proof Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28572)
*Rodrigo Mendoza-Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Lean 4内核级别的选择公理依赖追踪，将Mathlib中471,260个声明按对选择公理的传递依赖进行划分，对42,355个定理的tactic调用序列进行分析。研究发现：选择公理在证明空间中存在可测量的几何对应物，服从单参数混合律，并对神经定理证明器产生实际影响。具体表现为：(1) 三个互补测量指标（异常分数、重构损失、密度超水平包含）随证明与选择公理在依赖图中的距离增加而共同衰减，从浅层边界的显著分离（AUC 0.847，距离2）到距离9+时不可区分；(2) 在251个Mathlib定理的评估样本上，Lean的aesop策略解决构造性定理的效率是经典定理的13倍，神经引导混合方法将差距缩小至5倍；(3) 几何异常分数可预测aesop失败，且超越证明长度的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择公理在数学基础中引发了超过一个世纪的争议，但经典证明与构造性证明之间的区分一直停留在哲学和方法论层面。本文旨在通过Lean 4的形式化系统，从可测量的角度揭示选择公理在证明空间中的几何对应物，并探索其对神经定理证明器的操作化影响，从而将这一区分从哲学问题转化为可实证研究的科学问题。

Method: (1) 对Mathlib中471,260个声明按对选择公理的传递依赖进行分区，筛选出42,355个定理，用其tactic调用序列表示；(2) 使用构造性证明训练自监督证明编码器；(3) 用该编码器度量经典证明，采用三种互补测量指标（异常分数、重构损失、密度超水平包含）；(4) 进行鲁棒性控制（长度、文件、作者、主题控制）及全源编码器复制验证；(5) 在251个定理样本上评估aesop策略和神经引导混合方法（ReProver）的性能；(6) 分析几何异常分数对aesop失败的预测能力。

Result: (1) 三个测量指标随证明与选择公理在依赖图中的距离增加而共同衰减：浅层边界（距离2）AUC达0.847，距离9+时不可区分；(2) 鲁棒性控制在长度、文件、作者、主题控制后签名仍然存在，并在全源编码器上得到复制；(3) aesop策略解决构造性定理的效率是经典定理的13倍，神经引导混合方法（ReProver）将差距压缩至5倍；(4) 几何异常分数可预测aesop失败，且超越证明长度的预测能力。

Conclusion: 选择公理在形式化证明空间中存在可测量的几何对应物，这种几何签名具有操作化后果——它影响自动定理证明器和神经引导证明器的性能。这一发现将经典/构造性证明的区分从哲学和方法论层面扩展到了可实证研究的科学层面，为理解选择公理在形式化数学中的实际影响提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The axiom of choice has divided the foundations of mathematics for over a century, but the distinction between classical and constructive proofs has remained a philosophical and methodological one. We use Lean 4's kernel-level tracking of axiom dependence to show that the axiom of choice has a measurable geometric correlate in proof space that obeys a one-parameter mixture law and has operational consequences for neural theorem provers. To do this, we partition $471{,}260$ declarations of Mathlib by transitive dependence on the axiom of choice and represent a filtered population of $42{,}355$ traced theorems by their sequences of tactic invocations. We use the constructive proofs in this dataset to train a self-supervised proof encoder and show that when using it to measure classical proofs, three complementary measurements (anomaly score, reconstruction loss, and density-superlevel containment) exhibit a common decline with the proof's distance from the axiom in the dependency graph, from sharp separation at the shallow boundary (AUC $0.847$ at distance $2$) to indistinguishability at distance~$9{+}$. Robustness controls show that the signature survives length, file, author, and topic controls, and replicates under full-source encoders trained on normalised proof source. Operationally, we show that on an evaluation sample of $251$ Mathlib theorems, Lean's \texttt{aesop} tactic solves constructive theorems at $13\times$ the rate of classical ones, and a neural-guided hybrid using the ReProver tactic generator compresses the gap to $5\times$. The geometric anomaly score predicts \texttt{aesop} failure beyond proof length, providing an operational link between the geometric signature and prover performance.

</details>


### [134] [Replica Symmetry Breaking and Algorithmic Thresholds in Empirical Risk Minimization under Multi-Index Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28573)
*Andrea Montanari,Kangjie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在高维渐近框架下，研究了监督学习中经验风险最小化（ERM）的地形可访问性。通过提出增量近似消息传递（IAMP）算法，精确刻画了其在训练误差及测试与训练误差关系上的表现，并指出该算法在多项式时间内可能达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习模型通过优化高维非凸经验风险函数进行训练，这类函数存在大量局部最优解，但基于梯度的优化却能收敛到近乎全局最优。本文旨在精确理解哪些部分的经验风险地形能够被多项式时间算法所访问，从而解释这一现象。

Method: 在监督学习框架下，假设特征向量为标准高斯分布，响应变量依赖于其在未知k维子空间上的投影。通过经验风险最小化学习依赖于数据m维投影的模型（如m神经元神经网络）。提出增量近似消息传递（IAMP）算法，并在高维渐近（n,d→∞, n/d→α∈(0,+∞)）下精确刻画其训练误差及测试与训练误差的关系。

Result: 在高维渐近下，精确刻画了IAMP算法所实现的训练误差，以及测试误差与训练误差之间的关系。

Conclusion: 基于相关模型的先前工作，本文预期IAMP算法在多项式时间算法中性能是最优的。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models are trained by optimizing high-dimensional non-convex empirical risk functions. Such cost functions can have a multitude of local optima and yet, gradient-based optimization appears to converge to near-global optima.
  Within a simple supervised learning setting, we develop a precise picture of which parts of the empirical risk landscape are accessible by polynomial-time algorithms. We are given i.i.d. pairs $\{(\boldsymbol{x}_i,y_i):\; 1 \le i\le n\}$ with $\boldsymbol{x}_i\in \mathbb{R}^d$ standard Gaussian feature vectors, and $y_i\in\mathbb{R}$ response variables that depend on $\boldsymbol{x}_i$ through their projections on an unknown $k$-dimensional subspace. We use empirical risk minimization to learn a model that depends on an $m$-dimensional projection of the data (e.g., an $m$-neurons neural network).
  We propose an incremental approximate message passing (IAMP) algorithm and precisely characterize the training error it achieves, as well as the relation between test and training error, in the high dimensional asymptotics $n,d\to\infty$, with $n/d\toα\in (0, +\infty)$. Based on earlier work in related models, we expect that the performance achieved by our algorithm is optimal among polynomial-time algorithms.

</details>


### [135] [What LLMs explain is not what they believe: Evaluating explanation sufficiency under models' own input beliefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28615)
*Nhi Nguyen,Shauli Ravfogel,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自洽充分性（SCSuff）指标，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）自由文本解释的充分性，证明解释充分性依赖于输入分布，并通过实验发现LLM的解释普遍不充分，且与模型大小、准确率或输出熵弱相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险场景中，LLM常使用链式思维或事后理由等自由文本解释来证明模型输出，但这些解释是否包含足够信息来说明模型输出的生成过程尚不清楚。现有充分性定义针对特征归因，未能推广到任意解释，且LLM解释的充分性可能随输入分布变化。

Method: 1) 将经典充分性从特征归因推广到任意解释；2) 证明解释充分性依赖于输入分布，需为LLM解释显式定义；3) 利用LLM自身根据解释生成替代输入，捕捉其对可能输入的信；4) 提出自洽充分性（SCSuff），一个信息论指标，无需预定义偏或捷径即可评估自由文本解释。

Result: 实验表明：SCSuff与适用的针对性扰动测试结果一致；解释充分性随输入分布变化；LLM解释普遍不充分；充分性与模型大小、准确率或输出熵弱相关。最后隐层状态分析显示，高/低SCSuff得分可从内部表征预测。

Conclusion: SCSuff可用于检测和改进充分的LLM解释，指导高风险场景中更可靠的解释生成。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains, where free-text explanations such as chain-of-thought and post-hoc rationales are used to justify model outputs. Yet it remains unclear whether these explanations are sufficient, i.e., if they contain enough information to explain the model's output-generating process. We generalize classical sufficiency from feature attributions to arbitrary explanations and prove that explanation sufficiency can change depending on the input distribution, which must be explicitly defined for LLM explanations. We propose using the LLM itself to generate alternative inputs conditioned on an explanation, capturing its beliefs about possible inputs. We formalize self-consistent sufficiency as a goal for free-text explanations and introduce an information-theoretic metric, SCSuff, that enables evaluation of free-text explanations without relying on predefined biases or shortcuts. Our experiments show that SCSuff agrees with targeted perturbation tests where applicable and demonstrate that explanation sufficiency can vary with the input distribution. We find LLM explanations are generally insufficient and weakly correlated with model size, accuracy, or output entropy. Analysis of final-token hidden states shows that top and bottom SCSuff scores can be predicted from internal representations, suggesting that SCSuff can guide detection and improvement of sufficient LLM explanations. The code for this paper is available at https://github.com/rajesh-lab/self-consistent-sufficiency .

</details>


### [136] [Randomized Exploration for Linear Bandits via Absolute Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28616)
*Toshinori Kitamura,Shuai Liu,Csaba Szepesvári*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出绝对汤普森采样（ATS）及其集成版本EATS，通过将探索噪声取绝对值来确保期望乐观性，在保留计算效率的同时简化了遗憾分析，实现了与TS相当的$ \tilde{O}(d^{3/2}\sqrt{K})$遗憾界，且EATS在大集成规模下可恢复UCB行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UCB算法遗憾分析简单但计算成本高，TS计算高效但分析复杂（需反集中论证）。本文旨在弥合随机探索与确定性乐观主义之间的鸿沟，提出一种既计算高效又易于分析的方法。

Method: 提出ATS：将TS中的符号化探索噪声替换为其绝对值，确保期望乐观性。进一步提出EATS：对多个绝对扰动取最大值并除以集成规模归一化，随集成规模增大逐渐趋近UCB目标。

Result: ATS实现$ \tilde{O}(d^{3/2}\sqrt{K})$遗憾界，与现有TS在线性bandit中的界匹配。实验表明中等集成规模的EATS已能取得强劲性能。

Conclusion: ATS和EATS在理论上和实践中均建立了随机探索与确定性乐观主义之间的桥梁，兼顾了计算效率与分析简洁性。

Abstract: In stochastic linear bandits, the canonical Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) algorithm admits a simple frequentist regret analysis but can be computationally demanding, while Thompson Sampling (TS) is computationally attractive yet typically harder to analyze due to its non-optimistic nature. We propose Absolute Thompson Sampling (ATS), a simple modification of TS that ensures optimism in expectation by replacing the signed exploration noise with its absolute value. This preserves the computational efficiency of TS while avoiding the technically involved anti-concentration arguments common in TS analyses, enabling a simple UCB-style regret analysis. We show that ATS achieves $\tilde{O}(d^{3/2}\sqrt{K})$ regret, matching existing bounds for TS in linear bandits. We further introduce Ensemble Absolute Thompson Sampling (EATS), which takes the maximum over multiple absolute perturbations with normalization by the ensemble size. As the ensemble size grows, EATS converges to the UCB objective, recovering UCB behavior in the limit. Experiments show that moderate ensemble sizes already yield strong performance. Our results point to a bridge between randomized exploration and deterministic optimism both in theory and practice.

</details>


### [137] [Improving Patient Subtyping on Longitudinal Data using Representations from Mamba-based Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28623)
*Md Mozaharul Mottalib,Rahmatollah Beheshti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自监督Mamba的模型，用于从电子健康记录（EHR）数据中学习有效表示，以实现患者子类型划分（聚类）。在公开和私有EHR数据集上的实验表明，该模型在预测任务上优于基线模型，并能通过聚类技术对患者进行有效分型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用EHR数据进行患者子类型划分（聚类）对精准医疗具有重要意义，但由于EHR数据固有的复杂性（如时间不规则性、高维稀疏性），子类型划分任务面临挑战。

Method: 提出基于自监督Mamba的模型，学习EHR表示；结合聚类技术对学习到的表示进行患者子类型划分。

Result: 在公开和私有真实EHR数据集上，模型在预测任务上优于竞争基线模型；聚类分析表明所学表示能有效进行患者子类型划分。

Conclusion: 自监督Mamba模型能够有效处理EHR数据的复杂性，提升了患者子类型划分的效果，为精准医疗提供了有价值的工具。

Abstract: Effective sub-typing (also known as grouping or clustering) of patients using their electronic health record (EHR) data can greatly inform precision medicine efforts. However, subtyping temporal EHR datasets is known to be challenging due to inherent EHR issues, including complexity and irregularity. In this study, we propose a self-supervised Mamba-based model that learns effective EHR representations and enables enhanced patient subtyping. We evaluate the proposed model on public and private real-world EHR datasets to classify the data based on the available labels and subtype patients based on the representations learned from the model. Through an extensive set of experiments, we demonstrate that our model's design choices lead to better performance compared to competitive baseline models for prediction. Moreover, we evaluate several clustering techniques to demonstrate that our findings offer valuable insights into subtyping patients based on temporal records from EHR models\footnote{Our implementations are available at https://github.com/healthylaife/triplet_mamba.

</details>


### [138] [When More Sampling Hurts: The Modal Ceiling and Correlation Ceiling of Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28661)
*Yong Yi Bay,Kathleen A. Yearick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出语言模型在测试时扩展采样（test-time scaling）虽然能提升覆盖率（至少一次正确），但由于选择瓶颈（无法识别正确样本），额外采样反而可能使最终答案更差。文章提出了“可识别性差距”（identifiability gap）概念，并给出了一个“有效样本数”指标来动态确定最优采样终止点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理系统通过多次采样（test-time scaling）来提高正确率，但实际部署时只能返回一个答案，而模型无法识别哪次采样是正确的。因此，越多采样反而可能让模型对错误答案更自信，同时增加计算成本。作者希望量化这一现象，并找到一个合理的采样终止点。

Method: 通过理论分析和实验观察，定义了“可识别性差距”（覆盖率与选择正确率之间的差距），并提出了“模态天花板”（modal ceiling）和“相关天花板”（correlation ceiling）两种采样界限。进一步，给出了一个可计算的“有效样本数”指标，用于动态判断何时停止采样。

Result: 发现对于答案选择，投票结果在几十次采样后趋于稳定（模态天花板）；对于基准评分，收敛更早（相关天花板）。超过这些点后，额外采样不仅浪费计算资源，还可能降低最终答案质量。

Conclusion: 问题的关键不在于是否采样，而在于采多少；答案是不宜过多。瓶颈在于“识别”正确答案，而非“生成”正确答案。

Abstract: People overthink; language models over-sample, and the extra effort can talk both into a worse answer. Reasoning systems answer a hard question by sampling it many times (test-time scaling), and the more they draw, the more often a correct answer turns up somewhere, so coverage, the fraction of problems with at least one correct try, climbs and appears to be progress. But a deployed system must return one answer, and choosing it, not knowing which try is right, is selection; selection is capped, and past a point extra samples only make the model surer of a confident mistake, even as every draw adds cost. The gap between climbing coverage and stalled selection, the identifiability gap, is the answer a model can produce but not pick. So the real question is not whether to sample but how far, and the answer is: not far. For picking an answer, the vote has already settled within a few dozen draws, the modal ceiling; for scoring a benchmark, sooner still, the correlation ceiling. Beyond that, extra draws cost compute and add nothing, and can even make the answer worse. This paper turns the cutoff into a single number, the effective number of samples, that any sampling run already reveals. The bottleneck is recognizing a right answer, not generating one.

</details>


### [139] [Closed-Form Steepest Descent Direction toward Flat Minima: Reducing Upper Bounds on the Loss Hessian Eigenspectrum in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28662)
*Yuto Omae,Kazuki Sakai,Yohei Kakimoto,Makoto Sasaki,Yusuke Sakai,Hirotaka Takahashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对三隐层神经网络，通过解析推导交叉熵损失Hessian矩阵最大特征值的Wolkowicz-Styan上界的梯度，提出HSR正则化方法，沿该梯度最陡下降方向更新参数，以引导网络收敛到平坦最小值，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 损失景观平坦性与神经网络泛化能力相关，但现有方法多聚焦于过程设计，未阐明数据分布和网络结构如何决定平坦极小值的方向，且解析刻画这些方向在数学上通常难以处理。

Method: 解析推导了三层神经网络交叉熵损失Hessian矩阵最大特征值的Wolkowicz-Styan上界的梯度，并据此提出Hessian谱范围（HSR）正则化，沿该梯度的最陡下降方向更新参数。

Result: HSR正则化收窄了Hessian特征值谱，避开了尖锐极小值和鞍点，促进了收敛到平坦极小值。

Conclusion: 这是首个在无需数值近似的情况下，报告了促进收敛到平坦极小值的闭式梯度的工作，虽目前仅适用于交叉熵损失和三隐层架构，但其理论分析有望推动神经网络的进一步发展。

Abstract: The flatness hypothesis suggests that flatness of the loss landscape, as measured by the eigenvalues of the loss Hessian, correlates with better neural network generalization. While various algorithms reduce these eigenvalues, most focus on procedural design, leaving it unclear how data distributions and NN parameters structurally determine directions toward flat minima. Characterizing these directions analytically is generally intractable. To overcome this mathematical difficulty, recent studies derived the Wolkowicz-Styan (WS) upper bound on the maximum eigenvalue of the cross-entropy loss Hessian in three-layer NNs. Although this upper bound is differentiable, its gradient was not derived. Therefore, we analytically derive the gradient of the WS upper bound to characterize directions leading to flat minima. Based on this, we propose Hessian Spectral Range (HSR) Regularization, which updates parameters along the steepest descent direction of the WS bound. Experiments demonstrate that HSR Regularization narrows the Hessian eigenvalue spectrum, avoids sharp minima and saddle points, and promotes convergence to flat minima. Although the applicability of this method is currently limited to cross-entropy loss and three-layer architectures, to the best of the authors' knowledge, this is the first study to report a closed-form gradient that promotes convergence to flat minima without numerical approximations. Therefore, the theoretical analysis of this gradient is expected to contribute to the further development of NNs.

</details>


### [140] [Entropy Regularized Reinforcement Learning for Zero-Sum Stochastic Differential Games in a Regime-Switching Jump-Diffusion Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28669)
*Congde Hu,Zhuo Jin,Danping Li,Lin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对随机微分博弈中参数误设和结构突变问题，提出一种分布控制方法，将最优策略表征为状态和参数条件下的动作概率分布，构建了熵正则化零和随机微分博弈的强化学习框架，推导了HJBI方程，对线性二次问题给出半解析解，并开发了Actor-Critic算法，通过投资博弈实例验证了温度参数和体制转换的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机微分博弈框架存在参数误设（parameter misspecification）和突发性结构环境变化（sudden structural environmental changes）的问题，需要一种更鲁棒的控制方法。

Method: 1) 将最优策略表征为关于连续状态、离散体制状态和参数的概率分布（分布控制方法）；2) 构建熵正则化零和随机微分博弈（ERRL-ZSSDGs）强化学习框架，引入体制转换跳扩散过程；3) 利用动态规划原理（DPP）推导耦合HJBI方程组，均衡策略通过值函数梯度表达；4) 对线性二次问题，通过求解耦合常微分方程组得到半解析解；5) 对一般问题，开发Actor-Critic策略改进算法进行数值逼近。

Result: 在线性二次问题中获得了值函数和均衡策略的半解析解；在一般设置中Actor-Critic算法能够有效逼近不同体制下的值函数和均衡策略；投资博弈数值算例展示了温度参数和体制转换对最优策略和值函数的影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布控制方法能够有效应对参数误设和结构突变问题，将强化学习与随机微分博弈结合为复杂动态环境下的鲁棒策略优化提供了新途径。

Abstract: To address parameter misspecification and sudden structural environmental changes in conventional stochastic differential game (SDG) frameworks, this paper introduces a distributional control approach that characterizes optimal strategies as probability distributions over actions, conditioned on the continuous state, the discrete regime state, and parameters. This forms a reinforcement learning framework for entropy-regularized zero-sum stochastic differential games (ERRL-ZSSDGs) in a regime-switching jump-diffusion process. Using the dynamic programming principle (DPP), we derive the associated coupled systems of Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs (HJBI) equations, from which equilibrium strategies are expressed via gradients of the value function. For linear-quadratic problems, semi-analytical solutions for both value function and equilibrium strategies are obtained by solving a system of coupled ordinary differential equations (ODEs). In more general settings, an Actor-Critic policy improvement algorithm is developed to approximate the value functions and equilibrium policies across different regimes. The method is applied to an investment game, and numerical examples illustrate the effect of the temperature parameter and regime transitions on optimal policies and values.

</details>


### [141] [Entropy-Regularized Reinforcement Learning for Linear-Quadratic Stackelberg Differential Games in Regime-Switching Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28671)
*Congde Hu,Danping Li,Lin Xu,Wenying Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于熵正则化强化学习（ERRL）的方法来解决连续时间扩散框架下马尔可夫体制切换的线性二次Stackelberg微分博弈（LQ-SDG），通过推导弱耦合HJBI方程、集成神经网络近似值函数以及新颖的采样技术，有效解决了高维系统中的计算挑战，并利用探索性策略避免次优均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Stackelberg微分博弈（SDG）求解依赖于动态规划和Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs（HJBI）方法，在高维系统中计算复杂度过高，且经典方法容易陷入次优均衡。

Method: 提出熵正则化强化学习（ERRL）方法；推导带有熵正则化的探索性弱耦合HJBI方程；集成神经网络近似体制依赖的值函数以高效求解高维偏微分方程（PDE）；引入新颖的采样技术增强计算可行性。

Result: 数值实验表明，与经典方法相比，所提框架能有效逃离次优陷阱，在层次化决策问题中取得更优的鲁棒解。

Conclusion: 熵正则化与神经网络近似在应对环境突变的高维层次化决策问题中具有关键作用，为求解复杂连续时间SDG提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Stackelberg differential games (SDGs) provide a powerful framework for hierarchical decision-making in stochastic and continuous-time environments, yet their solution remains computationally challenging due to the complexity of traditional dynamic programming and Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs (HJBI) methods, especially in high-dimensional systems. This paper proposes an entropy-regularized reinforcement learning (ERRL) approach for linear-quadratic SDGs (LQ-SDGs) within a continuous-time diffusion framework governed by Markovian regime switching. The key innovation lies in deriving exploratory weakly-coupled HJBI equations with entropy regularization, which promotes stochastic policies that actively avoid suboptimal equilibria -- a limitation of classical SDG methods. Neural networks are integrated to approximate regime-dependent value functions and solve high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs) efficiently, while a novel sampling technique enhances computational tractability. Numerical results demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework compared to conventional approaches, particularly in escaping suboptimal traps through exploratory policies. The study highlights the critical role of entropy regularization and neural network approximations in achieving robust solutions for hierarchical decision-making problems under abrupt environmental shifts.

</details>


### [142] [Constrained Tabular Diffusion for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28674)
*Michael Cardei,Jose M Munoz,Oscar Barrera,Shreyas K Chandrahas,Partha Saha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CTDF，通过在反向扩散采样循环中集成无训练的可行性算子，实现对金融表格数据的硬约束生成，在大型金融数据集上达到零约束违规并提升稀缺数据效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融领域的生成模型需要同时满足数据真实性与严格的监管和经济目标约束，而标准表格扩散模型无法提供这种保证。

Method: 在反向扩散采样循环中引入无训练的可行性算子（feasibility operator），与混合类型表格扩散模型相结合，在采样时强制执行硬约束。

Result: 在大型金融数据集上实现零约束违规，并改善了稀缺数据的效用。

Conclusion: CTDF为生成可信且合规的金融合成数据提供了稳健方法，为金融领域的严格生成建模与分析开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Generative models in finance face the dual challenge of producing realistic data while satisfying strict regulatory and economic objectives, a requirement that standard tabular diffusion models cannot provide. To address this difficulty, we introduce Constrained Tabular Diffusion for Finance (CTDF), a novel integration of sampling-time feasibility operations with mixed-type tabular diffusion in financial applications. By incorporating a training-free feasibility operator into the reverse-diffusion sampling loop, CTDF enforces hard constraints for applications such as simulation, legal compliance, and extrapolation. Extensive experiments on large-scale financial datasets demonstrate zero constraint violations and improvement in scarce data utility. CTDF establishes a robust method for generating trustworthy and compliant synthetic data, opening new avenues for rigorous generative modeling and analysis in the financial domain.

</details>


### [143] [A Path-Space Formulation of Prediction in World Models: From a Single Action to Prediction, Planning, and Irreversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28751)
*Gunn Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于路径空间的AI世界模型预测框架，将世界模型隐式定义为未来轨迹上的概率测度。在有效马尔可夫潜动力学下，该路径测度呈现Onsager-Machlup形式，预测、规划和不确定性统一于作用量泛函操作。通过分解潜动力为可逆与不可逆分量，引入熵产生的操作度量。在小型注意力模型中发现，注意力不对称性与数据不可逆性成比例习得；对称化注意力会抑制熵产生并选择性降低不可逆动力学的长程预测能力，表明不可逆性可作为预测性世界模型的计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI世界模型主要基于单步条件分布的序列预测，缺乏对轨迹整体概率分布的建模视角。作者提出应从路径空间角度理解世界模型，将预测、规划和不确定性统一在同一数学框架下，并探索不可逆性在预测中的作用机制。

Method: 1) 提出路径空间公式，将世界模型定义为未来轨迹上的概率测度；2) 在马尔可夫潜动力学假设下推导Onsager-Machlup形式的路径测度；3) 将潜动力分解为可逆与不可逆分量；4) 从模型 rollout 中定义熵产生的操作度量；5) 在可控的小型注意力模型上实验验证注意力不对称性与数据不可逆性的关系。

Result: 1) 注意力不对称性在训练过程中与数据不可逆性成比例地习得；2) 对称化注意力会抑制熵产生；3) 对称化选择性降低了不可逆动力学的长程预测能力，但保留弛豫性预测。

Conclusion: 不可逆性可作为预测性世界模型的计算资源。更一般地，预测的基本对象应是未来路径上的分布而非状态上的分布。

Abstract: We propose a path-space formulation of prediction in AI world models. Rather than sequences of one-step conditional distributions, we argue that a world model implicitly defines a probability measure over future trajectories. In the local regime where latent dynamics admit an effective Markovian description, this path measure takes the Onsager-Machlup form. Within this framework, prediction (most probable trajectory), planning (constrained optimization), and uncertainty (fluctuations) emerge as operations on a single action functional.
